## what this list does NOT establish
#
# A marker in a file says the file contains that string. It does
# not say the component is unmodified, and two products carrying
# one vendor's marker at different sha1 are carrying two builds,
# not one library. That is exactly why the vendor column exists:
# the hashes will not cross and the vendor will.
